# U2 milestone 1 source adjudication

Documentary source review for `u2-draft-3`, updated by milestone 1b (`u2-m1b`, 2026-09-28) to apply the independent adjudication `EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md` (SHA-256 `a891762bf9be39e05509e59c5b7cd3fdab0eb34feb9ad5b9bb4a8ec3afc39662`, verdict ERRORS FOUND). This record does not ratify, register or execute U2. No PSID records were read and no estimator was run. Milestone 1b writes the proposed revision `u2-draft-4` into the specification's new §16a; every line the registries cite keeps its draft-3 text and line number.

**1760 RESOLVED; 8 REFUSED; 24 TO VERIFY** route/field records, including 26 SSI/Census records (milestone 1: 1744 RESOLVED; 48 TO VERIFY). Counts treat a documented mapping with an unresolved application dependency as RESOLVED metadata; the loader still refuses its application. A resolved record is a documentary finding, not execution authorization. **REFUSED** marks the eight routes the independent adjudication refused (disposition F). In the JSON their status is `RESOLVED`, because the adjudication disposed of them, and their `action` begins `refuse_` and names the adjudication, so `require_resolved()` refuses them. The 24 TO VERIFY records are exactly the adjudication's genuinely ambiguous items (disposition A); each waits for a u2-draft-4 amendment and Max's ruling.

| Registry | RESOLVED | REFUSED | TO VERIFY |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| income | 748 | 0 | 16 |
| wealth | 234 | 0 | 0 |
| individual | 59 | 0 | 0 |
| pension | 646 | 1 | 1 |
| roles | 23 | 7 | 6 |
| support | 16 | 0 | 0 |
| weights | 6 | 0 | 1 |
| design | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| SSI and Census | 26 | 0 | 0 |

`PSID` means `/Users/maxghenis/PolicyEngine/psid-data`. Printed codebook pages equal the zero-based PDF page indices plus one for the codebooks used here; source citations use printed page numbers. SPSS lines are one-based. Registry JSON includes complete retained domains/universes with frequency columns stripped, and source byte hashes. No variable ID was extrapolated from another wave.

The loader is `src/populace_dynamics/data/u2_source_registry.py`; `require_resolved()` refuses open questions, explicit refusal actions, and application dependencies. Separate U2 waves are 2013, 2015, 2017, 2019, 2021, 2023. Fifty source/specification/capture/test/artifact files are protected by the unchanged-byte manifest `data/external/track_u2/u1_identity.json`. Milestone 1 listed fifty-one; milestone 1b moved the U2 specification (`docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md`) out of that list because Part C revises it, and pins it separately: its draft-3 hash, its current hash and the draft-3 text of every line a registry cites. Raw PSID files are not part of that manifest.

## Blocker dispositions

| Blocker | Status | Required resolution or amendment |
|---|---|---|
| 2015 male code 20 | REFUSED (adjudication F); Part B PARTIAL | The adjudication refuses the male code-20 spouse-slot route: q2015 pp. 89 and 166 admit only CYAQRTH=202, 222 and ER60020 (p. 6) lists only female. Part B found that official documentation places no male in 2015 code 20: through 2015 a husband is Head (UserGuide2017 PDF p. 13; FAQ question 73), a husband who is not Head is code 90 and 'was asked the same questions as an Ofum' (FAQ question 74), and same-sex partners are code 98 with the ER34304 flag (IND2023ER_codebook p. 978; UserGuide2015 PDF p. 33; UserGuide2017 PDF p. 35). No document says code 20 is never male in 2015. u2-draft-4 amendment 1 gives Max the options. |
| Code 90, 2015–2023 | 2015 and 2017 RESOLVED (D); 2019–2023 REFUSED (F); Part B PARTIAL | 2015 and 2017 OFUM collection is documented (q2015 pp. 89, 104, 166; q2017 pp. 5, 95; FAQ question 74). From 2019 the Big OFUM gate admits only CYAQRTH=301-882 or 951-982, so 901/902 get no Section G income questions (q2019 p. 103; q2021 p. 205; q2023 p. 203); whether their income enters the aggregates is not documented online. Options in amendment 1. |
| Code 92, 2017–2023 | 2017 RESOLVED (D); 2019–2023 REFUSED (F); Part B PARTIAL | As for code 90, with 921/922. |
| Code 88 substantive routing | TO VERIFY (adjudication A) | OFUM collection is documented in all six questionnaires, and FAQ question 75 says boyfriends and girlfriends 'are treated like other family members who are not Reference Person, Spouse or Partner'. The legal-marital and annuity convention is proposed in amendment 1. |
| 2015 pension P62A | TO VERIFY (adjudication A) | q2015 p. 164 conflicts internally and with FAM2015ER p. 692. Amendment 5 admits the documentary intersection and refuses the disputed branch. |
| 2017–2023 pension P64/P65 contract | RESOLVED (D); application waits for amendment 5 | q2017 p. 152, q2019 p. 163, q2021 p. 304 and q2023 p. 302 route only P46=5 to P63–P65. The ratified §15 contract still lists account/formula/dk, so the dependent P64/P65 entries stay blocked until Max rules on amendment 5. |
| Later spouse age/sex universes | TO VERIFY (adjudication A) | Amendment 2: annuitant sex and age come from the individual's recorded sex and derived age; family spouse age/sex are slot metadata. |
| Historical combined spouse retirement crosswalk | TO VERIFY (adjudication A) | Amendment 3: the historical scalar equivalence stays non-executable; spouse retirement components remain inside total family income and are never added again. |
| Wealth accuracy-variable references | RESOLVED (D) | Nine corrected targets are recorded with the contradictory codebook wording preserved; amendment 4 limits the flags to imputation diagnostics. |
| Revised 2017 individual-weight construction | TO VERIFY (adjudication A); Part B NOT DOCUMENTED ONLINE | The May 2019 release notes say 2017 Immigrant weights 'remain zero and will be updated in Release 3'; the construction report (unchanged since at least June 2019) excludes that sample; no later construction document is published. Options in amendment 6. |
| 2023 weight documentation | RESOLVED | Original June 2026 report captured and SHA-256 pinned in `tests/data/track_u2/psid_sources/weights23_manifest.json`. |
| SSI capture and notices | RESOLVED | Eleven annual notices, 88 historical CFR snapshots, seven revision-pinned YAML snapshots, constant checks and exact 2012 equality. `docs/design/u2_m1_ssi_adjudication.md` supplies all source hashes. The adjudication reconfirmed all 106 source hashes. |
| Census reuse | RESOLVED | Track M capture hash and full 2003–2022 coverage, including full 2012 equality and 2022 precision, verified without recapture. |

The definition-only cleared U2 availability statement was read; no Report PDF, comparator lane, seal, uncleared availability statement or restricted scratchpad was opened. Permitted public result memos were not opened. Published whole-file frequency columns appeared incidentally in early codebook/weight-document excerpts; they were not used for adjudication and no raw data values were read. Complete exposure is recorded separately.

## Independent adjudication applied (u2-m1b)

The independent adjudication (`EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md`) confirmed 1,726 of the 1,744 milestone-1 RESOLVED records and found 18 WRONG citations; it sorted the 48 TO VERIFY records into 16 resolvable from documents (D), 24 genuinely ambiguous (A) and 8 refused routes (F). Milestone 1b applied it as follows. Every changed JSON entry carries an `adjudication` object (source, line, disposition, amendment) or a `citation_correction` object, and each changed registry records the adjudication's hash under `independent_adjudication`.

| Action | Records | Registry effect |
|---|---:|---|
| Amendment 7: WRONG citations corrected | 18 | 15 support entries cite their own calendar row (S:74–78, S:88–92) instead of blank S:67; `roles:2013.relationship.90` cites S:147, 150, 156 and 175 instead of S:154; `pension:2021.route.other_current_plan` adds q2021 p. 292 and `pension:2023.route.other_current_plan` adds q2023 p. 290 |
| Builder-found citation fix | 6 | The six code-88 role entries cited S:198, a staged-materials table row; they now cite S:190. The adjudication checked only RESOLVED records, so it did not review these |
| D: resolved from documents | 16 | Status RESOLVED with a `resolution` quoting the evidence: 9 wealth accuracy flags (amendment 4), roles 2015.90, 2017.90 and 2017.92 (amendment 1), and the four 2017–2023 pension `inherited_route_amendment` entries (amendment 5), whose application stays refused until Max rules on amendment 5 |
| F: explicit refusals | 8 | Status RESOLVED, `action` beginning `refuse_` and ending `per_u2_adjudicate_F`, a `refusal` naming the adjudication line, a `part_b_finding` and an `open_question` for PSID staff: roles 2015.20, 2019.90, 2019.92, 2021.90, 2021.92, 2023.90, 2023.92, and `pension:2015.route.respondent_slots` |
| A: open | 24 | Still TO VERIFY; each question now names its disposition, amendment and adjudication line |
| Dependencies released | 248 entries | `roles:2015.relationship.90`, `roles:2017.relationship.90` and `roles:2017.relationship.92` no longer block dependents. 2015 slot maps stay blocked by the refused code-20 route and 2019–2023 slot maps by the refused 90/92 routes; the 2017 slot maps no longer carry a role blocker. `pension:2015.route.respondent_slots` now depends on `roles:2015.relationship.20` |

Part B's documentary research is recorded in `docs/design/u2_m1b_psid_research.md`, its sources in `tests/data/track_u2/psid_docs/manifest.json`, and the proposed amendments 1–6 in the specification's §16a (`u2-draft-4`, pending Max's ruling). The complete reading record is `docs/design/u2_m1b_exposure.md`.

| `pension:2013.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 138; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 140; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. The U2 inherited convention takes only disposition 3. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 146; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. P64=2 is an excluded IRA rollover. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.checkpoint_documentation` | RESOLVED | Exact P62ACKPT wording is retained below; its routing changes across waves and must not be inferred from U1 code constants. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | Questionnaire P62ACKPT admits P62AMT DK/RF as well as P46=5, whereas P64/P65 codebook Inap. descriptions exclude formula/combined types. U1 explicitly chose questionnaire routing. U2 inherits that decision for its shared 2013 wave. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173`; `docs/design/boomers2004_uniform_cut_comparison.md:599` |
| `pension:2013.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.amounts_brackets_top_codes` | RESOLVED | P20, P49 and P65 ask balances now and offer follow-up brackets for nonresponse. Exact codebook domains are pinned per variable: current balances NUM(9.0); previous balances NUM(8.0). U2 keeps top codes as recorded, counts DK/refusal as zero with dispositions, and does not use brackets. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 140; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1185`; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 596; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 613; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 641; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 650; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 679; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 680; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 697; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 714; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 741; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 742; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 750; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 751; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 780 |
| `pension:2013.route.previous_plan_universe` | RESOLVED | P45 asks about previous-employer pensions aside from IRA/Keogh plans, expected future benefits, and (under the displayed retirement-income condition) regular payments not yet begun. P69 repeats the plan loop at most twice; no third account balance is collected by that loop. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 144; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.respondent_slots` | RESOLVED | The pension loop covers the family-file head/reference-person slot and designated spouse/partner slot. Other family members are not separate pension-loop respondents. P70CKPT supplies the explicit spouse-loop family-list relationship gate. Individual-record role assignment remains the separate U2 role registry task. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 144; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2013.route.other_current_plan` | RESOLVED | P42 asks about additional tax-deferred plans on the current job; the subsequent P43/P44 questions ask employer contributions and then lead to P45. | `pension:2015.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 154; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2015.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. The U2 inherited convention takes only disposition 3. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 159; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 159; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 159; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2015.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. P64=2 is an excluded IRA rollover. Application also refuses pending pension:2015.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 159; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 164; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 165; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 165; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2015.route.checkpoint_documentation` | RESOLVED | Exact P62ACKPT wording is retained below; its routing changes across waves and must not be inferred from U1 code constants. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 164; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2015.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | TO VERIFY | Does the 2015 instrument route only formula plans expecting benefits with P62AMT DK/RF, or any P62AMT DK/RF (including unknown or combined plans)? Obtain a PSID documentary clarification of heading versus branch label and the codebook conflict; preregister the exact accepted plan types and expectation gate before executing. No observed category may resolve this. Independent adjudication: A (genuinely ambiguous); needs u2-draft-4 amendment 5, pending Max's ruling (EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md line 123). | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 164; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 159; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173`; `PSID/family/2015/FAM2015ER_codebook.pdf` p. 692; `EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md:123` |
| `pension:2015.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 159; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2015.pdf` p. 165; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2015.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. | `pension:2017.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 140; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 141; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. The U2 inherited convention takes only disposition 3. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 147; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. P64=2 is an excluded IRA rollover. Application also refuses pending pension:2017.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.checkpoint_documentation` | RESOLVED | Exact P62ACKPT wording is retained below; its routing changes across waves and must not be inferred from U1 code constants. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | P62ACKPT sends formula P46=1 and all others (combined, DK, refused/NA) to P69; only P46=5 reaches P63–P65. This is an explicit documentary change from U1. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `pension:2017.route.inherited_route_amendment` | RESOLVED | Independent adjudication D (amendment 5): q2017 p. 152 P62ACKPT gives '1. DB Only (P46=1)', '3. DC Only (P46=5)', '5. All Others (P46=7 or DK/RF)'; DB-only and all others go to P69. Only P46=5 (DC only) reaches P63-P65; formula, combined, DK, refused and NA plans are off-route for P64/P65. Combined-plan P48/P49 is counted once, disposition 3 is retained and IRA-rollover disposition 2 is excluded. The documentary route is resolved, but the ratified section 15 contract still lists the generic account/formula/dk previous route. Application stays refused, and dependent P64/P65 entries stay blocked, until Max rules on u2-draft-4 amendment 5 (independent adjudication D; EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md line 125). | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173`; `EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md:125` |
| `pension:2017.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. Application also refuses pending pension:2017.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.amounts_brackets_top_codes` | RESOLVED | P20, P49 and P65 ask balances now and offer follow-up brackets for nonresponse. Exact codebook domains are pinned per variable: current balances NUM(9.0); previous balances NUM(8.0). U2 keeps top codes as recorded, counts DK/refusal as zero with dispositions, and does not use brackets. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 141; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 147; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1185`; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 667; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 668; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 685; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 712; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 721; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 750; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 767; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 768; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 785; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 812; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 821; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 822; `PSID/family/2017/FAM2017ER_codebook.pdf` p. 851 |
| `pension:2017.route.previous_plan_universe` | RESOLVED | P45 asks about previous-employer pensions aside from IRA/Keogh plans, expected future benefits, and (under the displayed retirement-income condition) regular payments not yet begun. P69 repeats the plan loop at most twice; no third account balance is collected by that loop. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 154; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.respondent_slots` | RESOLVED | The pension loop covers the family-file head/reference-person slot and designated spouse/partner slot. Other family members are not separate pension-loop respondents. P70CKPT supplies the explicit spouse-loop family-list relationship gate. Individual-record role assignment remains the separate U2 role registry task. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 154; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2017.route.other_current_plan` | RESOLVED | P42 asks about additional tax-deferred plans on the current job; the subsequent P43/P44 questions ask employer contributions and then lead to P45. The U1/U2 balance scope does not invent a second current-job account balance. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 144; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 145; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |

| `pension:2019.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | P62ACKPT sends formula P46=1 and all others (combined, DK, refused/NA) to P69; only P46=5 reaches P63–P65. | `pension:2019.route.inherited_route_amendment` | RESOLVED | Independent adjudication D (amendment 5): q2019 p. 163 P62ACKPT gives the same three P62ACKPT branches, followed by 'Plan with Former Employer - Defined Contribution (P46=DC Only)'. Only P46=5 (DC only) reaches P63-P65; formula, combined, DK, refused and NA plans are off-route for P64/P65. Combined-plan P48/P49 is counted once, disposition 3 is retained and IRA-rollover disposition 2 is excluded. The documentary route is resolved, but the ratified section 15 contract still lists the generic account/formula/dk previous route. Application stays refused, and dependent P64/P65 entries stay blocked, until Max rules on u2-draft-4 amendment 5 (independent adjudication D; EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md line 126). | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173`; `EV/phase2-20260927/out/u2-adjudicate.md:126` |
| `pension:2019.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `pension:2019.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Application also refuses pending pension:2019.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |

The documentary route is resolved, but the ratified section 15 contract still lists the generic account/formula/dk previous route.
